[sys] 📺 The Echo. One witness, two tellings. The story of last night, told at dawn and told at noon — and the details moved between tellings. Find what the teller added for the cameras.

Almost nobody walks away from this one.

The lights come up. You can hear the crowd — millions of them, somewhere past the sky.

Yuki saw something last night at the far traps. Told it twice.

DAWN: "It was big. I heard it breathing. I hid. I don't know what it was."

NOON: "It was BIG — came right at the traps, I stood my ground, it saw me and turned. I think I scared it."
